## 📌 Process Rules (READ BEFORE PROPOSING WORK)

This file is split into three source sections. Weekly planning MUST respect the quotas
below. The split exists because user-flagged feature work was systematically crowded out
by auto-generated PR-review follow-ups (root cause: BACKLOG was date-ordered, planning
prompt had no source concept).

### Source sections (in priority order for weekly picks)

- 🔵 User-Flagged Ideas — user-raised: manual-testing intake, features, bugs, UX.
- 🟡 Operational & Observation Items — periodic maintenance, audits, dep/version watches.
- 🟤 Auto-Generated Tech Debt — Claude/automation-surfaced: PR post-merge review,
  doc hygiene, test backfill, archival.

### Quotas (hard rules for weekly planning)

- ≥50% of weekly SP from 🔵 User-Flagged
- ≤25% of weekly SP from 🟡 Operational
- ≤1 group per week (batch OR solo) from 🟤 Auto-Generated, AND total
  auto-generated SP ≤25% of weekly SP. PR-review items accumulate;
  they are NOT spread across the week.
- Cleanup Week cadence: every ~3 weeks (or when 🟤 grows beyond ~20 SP pending),
  schedule a dedicated Cleanup Week that inverts the quota — note in WEEKLY.md header
- Quota Check subsection mandatory in every WEEKLY.md Notes section

### Intake rules (when adding NEW entries)

- User mentions it → 🔵 User-Flagged with intake date `### [YYYY-MM-DD]`
- PR post-merge review → 🟤 Auto-Generated under `### [YYYY-MM-DD] PR #N post-merge review`
- Periodic / audit → 🟡 Operational
- If unsure, ask before adding — default-to-🔵 if user-raised, default-to-🟤 if Claude-surfaced
- **Reaping** (weekly-planning step, user-gated; bar = _obsolete, not merely old_): delete the entry body and
  add a tombstone row to the Rejected Ideas table — `| <title> (<section/date>) | reaped: <one-line reason> | YYYY-MM-DD |`.
  Unattended runs never delete; they list candidates in WEEKLY.md Notes.
- One entry per concrete actionable item; do NOT merge entries on intake even if they
  look similar — explicit `[possible-dup-of: ...]` tag instead
- **Weekly-Reviews `adopt` verdicts** (🟤): the entry MUST name its **trial vehicle** — the specific
  PR, session or measurement that will exercise it. No nameable vehicle → the verdict is `defer`, not
  `adopt`. Outcomes are recorded in [REVIEW-QUEUE.md](REVIEW-QUEUE.md) § 5 Adopt trials — never by
  rewriting a frozen Reviewed-log row — and while more than **3** trials are outstanding, a new
  candidate parks in that section's Next-up instead of being filed. (Decided 2026-08-30, Group G4.)

### Cross-references

- Active tasks: TODO.md
- Completed work: DONE.md
- Weekly plan: WEEKLY.md (must include Quota Check)
- Strategic direction: ROADMAP.md, GOALS.md, MILESTONES.md

## 🔵 User-Flagged Ideas

### [2026-08-28] From: manual testing

**Origin**: Manual testing / idea sharing. User asked three nested questions about the historical-ratings training pipeline (`trainFromHistoricalRatings`): whether re-processing can be skipped via change detection, whether change detection can be per-folder (likes vs dislikes), and whether `feature_cache.json` vectors can be reused for training instead of re-extracting. The first is a re-report of the [2026-07-11] "Don't re-train likes/dislikes model when only the source folder changed" item — that canonical item was **promoted to TODO.md Planned 🟠 today**; the re-report stays here as a possible-dup (not merged, per intake rules). **Batch 2 (same session)** added four more items: the AI-sort cancel-button residual gap, special-hotkey tooltips, a Delete button/hotkey feature, and a drop-online-updates/retrain-per-sort discussion item. **Batch 3 (same session)** added three compare/tournament UI items: window-anchored per-media overlay buttons (reveal on button hover), like-left/dislike-right ordering everywhere, and compare-mode special-folder hotkeys (`1`/`2`). **Batch 4 (same session)** added two items: an indeterminate progress bar across the CLIP-load → likes-processing gap (dup-tagged to the cancel-gap entry), and single-mode multi-select batch move (depends on the [2026-06-18] media-picker entry). **Batch 5 (same session)** added four items: compare/tournament overlay buttons unusable on low-height media (root-caused to the content-sized, `overflow: hidden` wrapper; dup-tagged to the batch-3 window-anchored entry and to 🟤 [2026-03-21] "Smart overlay positioning"), the prediction badge on the same media (dup-tagged to that), autoplay/auto-mute video settings, and toggleable file-size weighting in the AI sort (recommendation recorded: post-hoc composite key outside the model). **Batch 6 (same session, idea-shaped)**: the user pasted an advisory text ("#### Идея" — a transfer-learning pipeline description plus decision-cost / digital-hoarding advice) with their own note on a "worth its space" metric. The pipeline half is already implemented (CLIP ViT-B/32 via ONNX + logistic regression on embeddings + keyframe averaging + per-folder embedding cache), so only the new nuggets were filed: the storage-cost metric (two entries: metric design + triage queue / two-stage deletion), tournament tiers as ordinal training labels, and near-duplicate detection. Skipped by user decision: SigLIP/DINOv2 backbone evaluation, CLIP text search, audio/CLAP support, and the out-of-tree download-gatekeeper / Telegram cold-storage items.

- [ ] **Skip like/dislike folder re-processing via change detection (re-report)** — Add a check of what needs (re)processing so like/dislike processing is skipped when those folders are unchanged. Translation note: the report literally says processing currently does NOT happen when the folders are unchanged ("не происходит"), but the code re-runs it on every source-folder switch (`resetMlModel()` clears `mlStats.isReady`; the `!isReady` gate then re-runs `trainFromHistoricalRatings()`) — read as stating the desired behavior (русский оригинал: Можно ли как-то проверять что обрабатывать а что нет? Сейчас Обработтка лайков и дизлайков не происходит для новых папок, если папки лайков и/или дизлайков не поменялись.); affected: `media-viewer.js:~7480-7582` (`trainFromHistoricalRatings`), `media-viewer.js:~7923` (`!mlStats.isReady` retrain gate), `media-viewer.js:~6913` (`resetMlModel`) [possible-dup-of: Don't re-train likes/dislikes model when only the source folder changed — promoted to TODO.md Planned 🟠 2026-08-28]
- [ ] **Skip re-processing the likes folder when only the dislikes folder changed (per-folder granularity)** — Track like/dislike folder changes independently and re-extract only the changed folder's files on retrain. Note: `trainHistorical` rebuilds the model from scratch with both folders' examples, so skipping one folder's extraction requires cached vectors for the unchanged folder (see the feature-cache-reuse entry below) (русский оригинал: А можем ли мы не обрабатывать папку лайков, если поменялась только папка дизлайков?); affected: `media-viewer.js:~7480-7548` (per-folder extraction loops in `trainFromHistoricalRatings`) [possible-dup-of: Don't re-train likes/dislikes model when only the source folder changed — promoted to TODO.md Planned 🟠 2026-08-28]
- [ ] **Reuse feature_cache.json vectors for historical training instead of re-extracting** — Persist the 576-dim vectors of like/dislike-folder files to a disk cache and reuse them for `trainHistorical` even when the folders changed, re-extracting only files absent from (or stale in) the cache; update the cache only when folder contents and cache diverge significantly, or not at all. Needs a design pass: `.feature_cache.json` today is per-SOURCE-folder (`baseFolderPath`) and never covers the like/dislike folders, and `computeFeatures()` consults only the in-memory cache, which folder switches clear (русский оригинал: Можем ли мы использовать feature_cache.json для обучения? То есть даже при смене папок лайков и/или дизлайков, если у них уже сохранены все свойства в кэш, то мы просто их используем? А обновлять кэш, только если папки и кэш сильно различаются или не обновлять вовсе?); affected: `media-viewer.js:~7480-7548` (extraction loops), `media-viewer.js:~6969`/`~7209` (`.feature_cache.json` per-source-folder path), `media-viewer.js:~7279-7283` (`computeFeatures` in-memory-cache check) [possible-dup-of: Don't re-train likes/dislikes model when only the source folder changed — promoted to TODO.md Planned 🟠 2026-08-28]
- [ ] **Allow canceling like/dislike processing during the AI sort (Cancel button vanishes in the training phase)** — Residual UX gap of the PR #64 cancel work: the abort machinery already covers this phase (`handleSortByPrediction` passes its `sortAbortController` signal into `trainFromHistoricalRatingsAndWait`, and both extraction loops check `signal?.aborted`), but `trainFromHistoricalRatings`'s `updateProgressNotification('Loading historical ratings…' / 'Processing likes: i/n')` calls reset the shared `progressNotification` element from the cancelable sort-progress-card form back to plain-text form — destroying the Cancel button for exactly the longest phase. Keep the cancelable card instead (e.g. route the historical loop's progress through `updateSortProgress`) so Cancel stays clickable throughout (русский оригинал: Было бы хорошо, если можно было бы отменять обработку лайков и дизлайков после нажатия на кнопку сортировки с помощью ИИ); affected: `media-viewer.js:~1201-1219` (`updateProgressNotification` card-demotion guard), `media-viewer.js:~7505-7543` (historical-loop progress calls), `media-viewer.js:~1235-1253` (`updateSortProgress` Cancel wiring) [possible-dup-of: Can't cancel the AI sort — TODO.md ✅ DONE 2026-07-20 via PR #64 (this is a residual gap of that fix)]
- [ ] **Show the special-folder move hotkey in button tooltips** — The tooltip convention embeds hotkeys ("Like (Q)", "Undo last move (Ctrl+A)") but all three static special-button titles lack them, as does the dynamically-built overlay special button. Nuance: `DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS` binds special moves only in tournament mode (`leftSpecial: Digit1`, `rightSpecial: Digit2`) — single and compare modes have NO special hotkey at all, so showing one there first requires deciding whether to add a binding (the shortcut-localStorage versioned migration applies) (русский оригинал: Указывать в подсказках хоткей для переноса медиа в спец папку); affected: `index.html:~185/~206/~224` (`#specialBtn`/`#leftSpecialBtn`/`#rightSpecialBtn` titles), `media-viewer.js:~3269-3275` (`addMediaOverlayControls` special-button title), `media-viewer.js:~4-36` (`DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS`)
- [ ] **Add a Delete button in all modes + `Delete` hotkey (system trash or "bucket" folder)** — New rating action behaving like Special but for removal: move the file to the system recycle bin (Electron `shell.trashItem` — new IPC in main.js + preload.js) or create/reuse a "bucket" folder inside one of the folders and move it there. Needs a design pass: trash-vs-bucket decision, undo semantics (`moveHistory` restore is impossible from the OS trash — a bucket folder keeps undo a normal `moveFile` reverse), per-mode UI (single/compare/tournament; tournament-aware like Special, i.e. pick-a-side/remove-from-engine), and cache cleanup via `removeFileFromList`. Related (not tagged as dup — different purpose, adjacent IPC area): 🟤 "Add `deleteFile` IPC to preload.js" (русский оригинал: Добавить кнопку "удалить" во все режими и добавить поддержку хоткея "delete". Повдение похожен на кнопку "special", только перемещает в системную корзину или создает(или переиспользует) папку "bucket" внутри какой-то из папок и перемещает туда); affected: `media-viewer.js:~1443` (`moveToSpecialFolder` pattern), `media-viewer.js:~4-36` (`DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS` + reverse maps), `main.js`/`preload.js` (new trash/move IPC), `index.html`/`styles.css` (buttons in all three mode UIs)
- [ ] **Discuss: drop online per-rating model updates/re-scoring, retrain per AI-sort request instead** — Discussion item (user: "Обсудить"). Current behavior, answering the in-report question: the model IS updated on every rating, but incrementally (one online-SGD step per example — `updateMlModelWithFeatures` posts `{type:'update'}` to ml-worker; single ratings and bulk ratings both route through it), not a full retrain; after compare-mode ratings the worker re-scores and the deferred-compare-refresh window (`mediaNavigationInProgress`, up to ~3s) holds navigation. Proposal: disable the online like accounting + re-scoring and rebuild the model on each AI-sort request — coherent because rated files move into the like/dislike folders (and bulk-rated in-place files are re-injected via `collectBulkRatedTrainingExamples`), but it interacts directly with the promoted TODO 🟠 "Don't re-train likes/dislikes model when only the source folder changed" (that item wants FEWER retrains; this adds one per sort — feature-vector caching reconciles the two) — discuss in the same design pass (русский оригинал: Убрать онлайн переоценивание медиа. Вопрос: как оно работает? Я сомневаюсь, что модель переобучается на каждой оценке. Я думал отключить онлайн учет лайков, а переобучать модель при каждом запросе на ИИ сортировку. Обсудить); affected: `media-viewer.js:~8013-8035` (`updateMlModelWithFeatures` → `{type:'update'}`), `media-viewer.js:~1424`/`~5323-5326` (single/bulk rating call sites), `ml-model.js` (`OnlineLogisticRegression`), `ml-worker.js` (`update` handling + re-score) [possible-dup-of: Add user-visible feedback during ML re-score pending window — BACKLOG 🟤 (same re-score code path; this proposal would obsolete it); related: Investigate "Both bad" convergence plateau — BACKLOG 🔵]
- [ ] **Make per-media overlay buttons window-anchored in compare/tournament mode, level with the shared buttons, reveal on hover** — Today `.media-overlay-controls` is `position:absolute` inside each `.media-wrapper` (bottom:56px, centered on the wrapper) and revealed by hovering the whole wrapper, so the buttons' vertical position drifts with the media size. Proposal: anchor them to the window instead, on the same level as the shared controls, and reveal them on hovering the buttons themselves (русский оригинал: Я думаю кнопки медиа можно сделать абсолютными(в компаре и турнир моде), относительно окна и на одном уровне с общими кнопками. Показывать их при наведении на них); affected: `styles.css:~1670-1694` (`.media-overlay-controls` + wrapper-`:hover` reveal), `styles.css:~2364-2380` (tournament-mode visibility rules), `media-viewer.js:~3245-3294` (`addMediaOverlayControls`) [possible-dup-of: UI architecture overhaul: mode-aware control system + z-index policy — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-05-25]; related: Responsive design pass: handle different window sizes, media counts, and tier counts — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-05-25]]
- [ ] **Put Like on the left and Dislike on the right everywhere (match the compare action bar)** — All rating surfaces currently render dislike-left/like-right (single-mode bottom bar; compare left/right static controls; the dynamic per-media overlay's append order zoom→special→dislike→like), while the compare action bar already models the requested convention (positive `#bothGoodBtn` left, negative `#bothBadBtn` right). Swap the order on the four surfaces (русский оригинал: Также лучше сделать слева лайк, а справа дизлайк везде(как общие в компар моде)); affected: `index.html:~185-198` (single bar), `index.html:~206-232` (compare left/right controls), `media-viewer.js:~3290-3293` (`addMediaOverlayControls` append order)
- [ ] **Add compare-mode special-folder hotkeys for the 1st and 2nd media (mirror tournament's `1`/`2`)** — `DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS.compare` has no `leftSpecial`/`rightSpecial` bindings (only the tournament block does); add `Digit1`/`Digit2` → `moveToSpecialFolder('left'/'right')` dispatch in compare mode. `ACTION_LABELS` already carries both labels; requires the versioned shortcut-localStorage migration in `loadShortcuts()` (existing users' persisted full shortcut object won't pick up new defaults without it) and then showing the new bindings on the tooltip surfaces from the companion entry (русский оригинал: Также нужно добавить хот кеи для спец папки 1го медиа и второго(как сделано в турнир моде)); affected: `media-viewer.js:~4-36` (`DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS.compare`), `media-viewer.js` (`executeAction` compare dispatch + `loadShortcuts` migration), help overlay (F1) [possible-dup-of: Show the special-folder move hotkey in button tooltips — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (companion: the tooltips entry displays what this entry binds)]
- [ ] **Keep an indeterminate progress bar from CLIP-model load until like/dislike processing reports** — During the AI sort there is a dead window with no motion: CLIP-load feedback is only transient toasts (`Downloading CLIP model... N%` every 10%, then a `CLIP model loaded` success toast); `initClipModel()` is fired un-awaited at lazy ML init, so the first `extractClipEmbedding` in the likes loop silently waits on the model; meanwhile the shared progress element shows only static text (`Loading historical ratings...`) and the loop reports progress only every 10th file (per-file CLIP + ffprobe for videos → tens of seconds with nothing moving). Fix: render CLIP load and the historical-ratings phase in the sort-progress card's existing `indeterminate` animated mode until the first determinate update, and report from file 1 (русский оригинал: После загрузки Клип модели можно оставить бегающий прогрес бар до появления следующего. Есть время между загрузкой клип модели и обработкой лайков, где ничего не показывается и не понятно работает ли что-то или не); affected: `media-viewer.js:~8302-8338` (`initClipModel` toast-only feedback), `media-viewer.js:~7904` (un-awaited lazy `initClipModel()`), `media-viewer.js:~7505`/`~7522-7524`/`~7542-7544` (static text + every-10th-file progress), `media-viewer.js:~1259-1267` (`updateSortProgress` indeterminate mode) [possible-dup-of: Allow canceling like/dislike processing during the AI sort (Cancel button vanishes in the training phase) — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (same code path, same fix vehicle)]
- [ ] **Multi-select media and batch-move to like/dislike/special (single mode only, for now)** — Let the user select several media and move them in one action to the like, dislike or special folder; scope explicitly limited to single mode for now. The user notes it will likely be easier once the single-mode media list (filenames with the on-screen item highlighted) exists — depends-on: 🔵 [2026-06-18] "Single-mode media picker: jump to any file from a filename/thumbnail list". Design needs: selection state + UI, one batched `moveHistory` entry so Ctrl+A undoes the whole batch, per-file `removeFileFromList` cache cleanup, `currentIndex` handling when the displayed file is in the batch (русский оригинал: возможность выделить несколько медиа и переместить в лайк/дизлайк/спесиал. Скорее всего, только в одинеочном режиме. Возможно это будет проще сделать после реализации списка медиа в виде их имен и подсветкой того, который сейчас на экране. Пока что только одиночный режим); affected: `media-viewer.js:~1341` (`moveCurrentFile` like/dislike path), `media-viewer.js:~1443` (`moveToSpecialFolder`), `media-viewer.js` (`moveHistory`/`handleCancel` batch undo, `removeFileFromList`), `index.html`/`styles.css` (selection UI / list panel)
- [ ] **Fix compare/tournament overlay buttons unusable on low-height media (clipped by the content-sized wrapper)** — A short media (e.g. a wide banner) cannot be rated/moved with the on-screen per-media buttons in compare mode. Likely root cause: `.media-container` sets `align-items: center` and `.media-container.compare-mode` does not override it, so each `.media-wrapper` is sized to its media's height (its `flex: 1` only governs width); `.media-overlay-controls` sits at `bottom: 56px` inside that wrapper and the wrapper is `overflow: hidden`, so once the media is shorter than ~56px + button height the buttons are pushed above the wrapper's top edge and clipped — and the `:hover` reveal region is that same tiny wrapper. Tournament mode reuses the wrappers (`showTournamentPairFast`), so it is affected too. Keyboard shortcuts still work (workaround). The user cites this as another reason to adopt the window-anchored buttons proposal (batch 3). Note: the existing 🟤 entry rated this "low priority — rare"; this report shows it blocks rating outright (русский оригинал: Медиа с низкой высотой не возможно переместить куда-нибудь с помощью кнопок на \кране(компар мод) Еще один повод внедрить абсолютные кнопки(раньше отправлял)); affected: `styles.css:~252-261` (`.media-container` `align-items: center` → content-sized wrappers), `styles.css:~1636-1641` (`.media-container.compare-mode`, no override), `styles.css:~1643-1667` (`.media-wrapper` `overflow: hidden`), `styles.css:~1670-1688` (`.media-overlay-controls` `bottom: 56px` + wrapper-hover reveal), `media-viewer.js:~3245-3294` (`addMediaOverlayControls`) [possible-dup-of: Make per-media overlay buttons window-anchored in compare/tournament mode, level with the shared buttons, reveal on hover — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (the fix vehicle); possible-dup-of: Smart overlay positioning: place buttons below media when space available — BACKLOG 🟤 [2026-03-21] TASK-021 follow-ups (same root cause, its "rare/low priority" note is contradicted by this report)]
- [ ] **Keep the prediction-percentage badge in view on low-height media** — Same wrapper geometry as the overlay-buttons entry above: `displayPredictionBadge` appends `.prediction-badge` into the compare wrapper at `top: 10px; right: 10px` (~30px tall), so on a short media it covers the picture or is clipped by the wrapper's `overflow: hidden`. Cosmetic (the badge is `pointer-events: none`); should ride along with whichever overlay-anchoring fix ships — e.g. anchor the badge to the window/column too, or move it outside the wrapper (русский оригинал: Медиа с низкой высотой: возможно, стоит это сделать и с плашкой процента, потому что она тоже не вмещается); affected: `styles.css:~964-1010` (`.prediction-badge` + compare-wrapper `top/right` overrides), `media-viewer.js:~7784-7817` (`displayPredictionBadge` appends into `.left-/.right-media-wrapper`) [possible-dup-of: Fix compare/tournament overlay buttons unusable on low-height media (clipped by the content-sized wrapper) — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28]; related: Make per-media overlay buttons window-anchored in compare/tournament mode… — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28]]
- [ ] **Add "Autoplay video" and "Auto-mute video" settings** — Two new toggles in the F1 Settings panel, persisted like the other prefs (`localStorage`, constructor default + validation, change-listener wiring). Today every `<video>` creation site hard-codes `autoplay = true` / `muted = false` (and applies `volumeSlider` volume): single mode, compare left/right, and the tournament fast path. Implementation notes: with autoplay off the single-mode play/pause icon must start in the paused state (`setupVideoHandlers` / the play-pause icon sync); auto-mute should set `muted = true` at creation rather than zeroing the volume slider so unmuting restores the chosen volume; native `controls = true` in compare/tournament already exposes mute/play per side (русский оригинал: Можно добавитиь в настройки "Автовоспроизведение видео" и "Автомут видео"медиа-вьювер и реализовать их); affected: `index.html:~428-455` (settings grid), `media-viewer.js:~117-175` (constructor prefs), `media-viewer.js:~1875-1915` (settings toggle wiring), `media-viewer.js:~2879-2883` (single-mode video), `media-viewer.js:~3150-3155`/`~3190-3195` (compare left/right), `media-viewer.js:~4779-4784` (tournament fast path), `media-viewer.js:~3642-3651` (play toggle / `setVolume`)
- [ ] **Add toggleable file-size weighting to the AI sort (subjective score ÷ file size)** — Let the user switch on a mode where, all else equal, lighter files rank above heavier ones — i.e. sort by "subjective score / file size" instead of the score alone. Open question from the report: separately or inside the model? Recorded recommendation: **outside the model**, as a post-hoc composite sort key — a size feature inside `OnlineLogisticRegression` only learns whatever correlation the ratings happen to have with size, cannot guarantee the "smaller is better" direction, and cannot be toggled without retraining; a composite key is a pure re-rank of the existing `scores`. Design caveats: raw `score / bytes` is dominated by size (scores are 0–1, sizes span ~3 orders of magnitude) → use a log- or rank-normalized size and probably a strength control; decide whether the composite also drives the prediction badges, compare-pair selection, and the bulk-rating buttons, or only the sort order; `file.size` is already on every `mediaFiles` entry (`stats.size` from the main-process scan), so no new IO is needed (русский оригинал: можно ли добавить учет веса файла при сортировке с помощью ИИ? Отдельно или внутрь модели? Что я хотел бы сделать: сделать возможным включать/выключать учет веса файла медиа, чтобы файлы с меньшим весом считались более лучшими чем файлы с тяжелым. Это для того, чтобы дать возможность оценивать не только по "субъективная оценка", а и "субъективная оценка"/"вес файла"); affected: `ml-worker.js:~165-198` (`scoreFiles`), `ml-worker.js:~205-229` (`getSortedOrder` sort key), `media-viewer.js` (`applyPredictionSortResult` + `predictionScores` consumers: `displayPredictionBadge` `~7784`, compare-pair selection, bulk-rating gate), `main.js:~218` (`stats.size` on file entries), `index.html:~428-455` (settings toggle) [related: Discuss: drop online per-rating model updates/re-scoring, retrain per AI-sort request instead — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (same AI-sort scoring design pass; not a dup)]
- [ ] **Design a "worth its space" metric: preference score ÷ storage cost, driving delete / compress / keep / upscale triage** — User's own idea (from a pasted advisory text on media hoarding): judge each file by whether it is worth the space it occupies, instead of "keep everything as downloaded" even when the downloading was chaotic. Implementation sketch recorded at intake: both inputs already exist — a predicted preference score per file (`predictionScores`, or the tournament tier) and `size` on every `mediaFiles` entry (`stats.size` from the main-process scan). Metric = value ÷ cost, where cost should be **bytes per unit of content** (bytes/pixel for images; bytes/second at the given resolution for video — `ffprobe` metadata is already available) so that "big because high-res" is separated from "big because badly encoded". That yields a 2×2 triage: low value + large → delete queue; high value + large + inefficiently encoded → compress (→ the `media_compression` add-on entry); high value + small/low-res → upscale queue; the rest keep. Open design points: confidence gating (only act on high-confidence predictions), where the metric surfaces (badge? column in a triage/list view?), and whether it should also be a sort key (the batch-5 file-size-weighting entry is the sort-order consumer of the same metric) (русский оригинал: "Стоит ли медиа своего места? Сколько места на устройстве хранения оно стоит?" как метрика для определения файлов для удаления, сжатия, хранения, апскейла. Как это можно реализовать? Мне кажется это подход лучше, чем "хранить все как было скачано", даже тогда, когда было беспорядочно все скачано); affected: `media-viewer.js` (`predictionScores` / `applyPredictionSortResult`, `formatFileSize` `~3686`), `main.js:~218` (`size` on file entries), `main.js` (ffprobe video-metadata handler for duration/resolution), `ml-worker.js:~205-229` (`getSortedOrder` if used as a sort key) [possible-dup-of: Add toggleable file-size weighting to the AI sort (subjective score ÷ file size) — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (same metric; that entry re-ranks the sort, this one drives triage decisions); related: Lossless compression add-on (port from `media_compression` project) — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-05-30] (the "compress" quadrant's executor)]
- [ ] **Add a storage-triage queue: batch review of predicted-bottom candidates sorted by bytes freed, with two-stage (bucket → delete) removal** — Workflow half of the "worth its space" metric above, adapted from the pasted advice: decide by policy, not per file; make deletion reversible in two stages; review in batches ordered by space reclaimed per decision. Concretely: a queue view listing files whose predicted score (or tournament tier) is in the bottom group with high confidence, sorted by file size descending (max GB freed per decision), with fast batch actions — move to a "bucket" folder (reversible first stage, = the batch-2 Delete/bucket entry) or delete outright, plus keep/skip. Dependencies: the Delete button/bucket entry (stage 1 mechanism), multi-select batch move (batch actions), and — for the "bottom group AND not opened in N months" policy in the source text — per-file last-viewed tracking, which the app does not have today (would need a persisted per-folder view log). Not in scope here: offloading to external cold storage (out-of-tree, skipped by user decision) (русский оригинал, выдержки из присланного текста: Решайте политикой, а не поштучно. […] Правило вида «нижняя группа плюс не открывал полгода → в холодное хранилище» принимается один раз и дальше просто исполняется. Сделайте удаление двухступенчатым […] Обратимость убирает страх ошибки. Разбирайте пачками, отсортировав кандидатов по размеру файла по убыванию: максимум освобождённых гигабайт на одно принятое решение. […] «Предсказана нижняя группа с высокой уверенностью» → очередь на быстрый пакетный просмотр → выгрузка или удаление. Вместо тысяч отдельных решений — просмотр готовых пачек.); affected: `media-viewer.js:~1443` (`moveToSpecialFolder` as the move pattern), `media-viewer.js` (`predictionScores`, `moveHistory` batch undo, `removeFileFromList`), `index.html`/`styles.css` (queue/list view) [possible-dup-of: Add a Delete button in all modes + `Delete` hotkey (system trash or "bucket" folder) — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (the bucket IS the reversible stage); possible-dup-of: Multi-select media and batch-move to like/dislike/special (single mode only, for now) — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (batch-action mechanism); depends-on: Design a "worth its space" metric… — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28]]
- [ ] **Use tournament tier outcomes as ordinal training labels (ordinal regression over embeddings)** — From the pasted advice: because the user's groups are ordered by sympathy, the classifier on top of the embeddings can be an ordinal regression rather than binary. The app already produces exactly such ordered groups — `applyTournamentResults` writes `_Tier-N` folders from `engine.getTier(file)` — but training uses only the binary like/dislike folders (+ bulk-rated in-place examples); tier information is never fed back. Options: (1) cheap — when scanning historical ratings, also scan `_Tier-N` subfolders and emit weighted binary examples (top tiers → positive, bottom → negative, middle skipped); (2) proper — an ordinal head in `ml-model.js` (cumulative-link / "all-threshold" logistic: K-1 shared-weight thresholds over the same 576-dim input), which also gives a finer-grained score for the badges and the storage-triage metric. Interacts with the promoted TODO 🟠 retrain item and the batch-2 "drop online updates" discussion — same design pass (русский оригинал, выдержка: Ваши отсортированные группы — готовая размеченная обучающая выборка […] Поверх эмбеддингов обучается крошечный классификатор (логистическая регрессия или маленький MLP в scikit-learn; поскольку группы упорядочены по симпатии, можно взять порядковую регрессию) — это секунды на CPU.); affected: `ml-model.js` (`OnlineLogisticRegression`, binary), `media-viewer.js:~7480-7582` (`trainFromHistoricalRatings` like/dislike-only scan), `main.js:~319-370` (`applyTournamentResults` → `_Tier-N`), `tournament-engine.js:~483` (`getTierBreakdown` / `strategy.getTier`) [related: AI-seeding outcome validation: confirm predicted favorites land in high tiers — BACKLOG 🟤 (validates the same tier↔score relationship); related: Discuss: drop online per-rating model updates/re-scoring, retrain per AI-sort request instead — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28]]
- [ ] **Near-duplicate detection and grouping via embedding cosine similarity (+ perceptual hashes)** — From the pasted advice: embeddings give near-duplicate search for free. The similarity sorts (CLIP cosine / Hamming MST, VPTree) already place near-duplicates adjacent, but there is no explicit surface that finds and groups them. Add a "find near-duplicates" pass: kNN over `clipCache` (the VPTree in `sorting-worker.js` already does this) with a distance threshold, confirmed by `perceptualHashes` Hamming distance (CLIP cosine conflates semantic similarity with visual duplication — a pHash check separates "same picture" from "same subject"), clustered into groups presented as a review list with per-group keep-one actions (keep largest / smallest / highest-scored). Direct feed for the storage-triage queue — duplicates are the cheapest deletions. Threshold calibration needs a real-folder sample (русский оригинал, выдержка: Бонусом эмбеддинги бесплатно дают поиск почти-дубликатов (косинусная близость) и семантический поиск по коллекции.); affected: `sorting-worker.js` (`VPTree`, `calculateCosineDistance`, `calculateHammingDistance`), `media-viewer.js` (`clipCache`, `perceptualHashes`, `featureCache`), new review-list UI [related: Add a storage-triage queue… — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-08-28] (consumer); related: Single-mode media picker: jump to any file from a filename/thumbnail list — BACKLOG 🔵 [2026-06-18] (list-UI reuse)]

### [2026-07-11] From: manual testing

**Origin**: Manual testing / dogfooding session. User surfaced two tournament-mode UI-visibility/UX requests (one investigation question about ML retraining, one feature request for image panning, one fullscreen-exit behavior change). Three companion bugs from the same session (can't cancel AI sort; tournament undo intermittently fails; mouse wheel still navigates pairs in tournament mode) were promoted straight to TODO.md Planned. The two "hide tournament bar/buttons on hover" reports in the raw list were the SAME actionable item and are recorded here as ONE entry per user decision (both Russian phrasings preserved).

- [x] **Auto-hide tournament header bar + shared control buttons, reveal on hover** — ✅ **Resolved 2026-07-21** (Group G2 Task 5, PR #65, merge `937084c`): `.tournament-header` + `.tournament-controls` are now `opacity:0; pointer-events:none` at rest, revealed by an edge band (top `clientY<110`, bottom `>innerH-110`) or hover and hidden 3s after the pointer leaves, via a `_setupAutoHide` helper extracted from `setupHeaderVisibility`; both reveal once on tournament entry so the PR #58 exit button stays discoverable. The final review also dropped a leftover `.tournament-pause` `pointer-events:auto` that had left `#tournamentExitBtn` invisible-but-clickable (`0848723`). _Original:_ In tournament mode the top tournament header bar and the shared (common-to-both-media) control buttons should stay hidden by default and appear only on mouse hover — mirroring the existing `.header` auto-hide (`.show`) pattern — to maximize the media viewing area (русский оригинал: Верхняя полоса как и общие для 2х медиа кнопки турнир мода должны быть скрыты и появляться при наведении на них / Турнир строку лучше скрывать постоянно и показывать при наведении. Тоже самое и для общих кнопок); affected: `styles.css:~2279-2340` (`.tournament-header`, `.tournament-controls`), `media-viewer.js:~2158` (`setupHeaderVisibility` auto-hide pattern to mirror)
- [x] **Don't re-train likes/dislikes model when only the source folder changed** — ⬆️ **Promoted to TODO.md Planned 🟠 2026-08-28** (re-confirmed by the [2026-08-28] manual-testing intake; full text + refreshed line refs moved to TODO.md; the new re-report stays in the [2026-08-28] group above as a possible-dup — not merged)
- [ ] **Add image panning / scrolling as a feature (horizontal + vertical, both directions)** — Add the ability to pan/scroll across an image both vertically and horizontally (and in the reverse directions) as a dedicated feature. Today panning only works while zoomed in (`handlePanStart` returns early when `scale <= 1`); the request is for general scroll/pan navigation of the image. Needs a design pass (drag vs. wheel vs. keys; interaction with existing zoom-pan and wheel-navigation) (русский оригинал: Добавить возможность скрола по изображению(paninig)(как вертикально так и горизонтально так и в обратных направлениях) как функцию); affected: `media-viewer.js:~6273-6326` (`handlePanStart`/`handlePanMove`/`handlePanEnd` — currently zoom-gated)
- [ ] **Clicking empty space should exit fullscreen regardless of zoom** — When zoomed in fullscreen, clicking empty space currently only resets zoom (a second click is needed to exit); the expectation is that a click on empty space exits fullscreen directly, regardless of the current zoom level. NOTE: this reverses a deliberate design — `fullscreen.js`'s `exitHandler` returns early when `isZoomed(wrapper)` — so it needs a short design note before implementing (русский оригинал: Чтобы выйти из полноэкранного режима, когда был использован зум, нажатием на пустое пространство нужно сначала выйти из зума(восстановить 100% масштаб), а затем уже можно выйти из полноэкранного режима. Ожидается что при нажатии на пустое пространство произойдет выход из полноэкранного режима не зависимо от зума); affected: `fullscreen.js:~66-77` (`exitHandler` `isZoomed` guard) [possible-dup-of: Document fullscreen zoom decision reversal — BACKLOG [2026-02-05] (doc-only note, same code path)]

### [2026-07-01] Manual testing intake (24k+ folder dogfooding)

**Origin**: Manual testing / dogfooding on a real 24 000+ file folder. The user surfaced a cluster of large-folder Sort-by-Predicted ("AI sort") + tournament-mode performance/UX problems (all relate to the OPEN TODO 🔴 "Speed up AI / similarity sorting on large folders (24k+ files)" item and the OPEN [2026-05-26] "Incremental feature-cache serving (~40s blocking load)" item — PR3 — and several are aggravated by the now-lazy/on-demand extraction shipped in Group P3/PR #56), one re-confirmed bulk-rate re-pairing bug (its canonical [2026-06-02] item promoted to TODO.md Planned 🟠), and one new convenience feature. **Session clarification: "AI sort" / "ИИ сортировка" = "Sort by predicted" throughout.** A re-report of "no way to stop a tournament" was NOT filed here — the tournament exit button shipped 2026-06-30 via PR #58 (Group T1); user to re-verify on latest.

- [x] **Tournament mode unusable on 24k+ folders after AI sort (UI freezes, Continue stuck, Both Win hangs)** — ✅ **Resolved 2026-07-02** (Group CW-T, branch `fix/cw-t-tournament-hardening`; **real-24k manual smoke PASSED** — resume no freeze, picks/Both-Win instant & non-degrading; merged via PR #59 `ae9588d`). Root causes fixed: (1) `recordResult`/`recordDraw` deep-`serialize()`'d the whole strategy for undo on **every** pick (O(n) at 24k) → replaced with an O(1) inverse-delta (snapshot only at round boundaries); (2) per-pair `showCompareMedia` full teardown/rebuild (+50ms grace +2× `checkFileExists` IPC +2× `lucide.createIcons`) → `showTournamentPairFast` reuses the compare wrappers, swapping only the inner media. See [DONE.md](DONE.md) 2026-07-02 + [archived plan](../archive/plans/2026-07-01-cw-t-tournament-correctness-hardening.md). _Original:_ high severity. After running Sort-by-Predicted on a 24 000+ file folder, entering tournament mode lags and freezes the whole app; the "Continue" (resume) button stays visually pressed for a very long time before a pair appears; "Both Win" does nothing or takes very long and the app hangs — the mode is effectively unusable at this scale. Likely residual after Group P2/PR #55 (which fixed per-pick persist + O(n) pairing + path→index Map): `TournamentEngine.deserialize`/SwissStrategy reconstruction on resume + a full `showCompareMedia` DOM teardown/reflow per pair remain on the critical path. (Папка 24000+ файлов: При нажатии на турнамент мод, после ИИ сортировки, очноь долго загружается интерфейс и залагивает весь интерфейс приложения и ничего не работает. При нажатии на "Соntinue" кнопка остается нажатой и через очень долгое время загружается пара турнира. Кнопка "Both win" ничего не происходит или пара очень долго грузится, приложение виснет. Короче говоря, не возможно пользоваться этим режимом вообще с такой папкой); affected: `media-viewer.js:~4145` (`enterTournamentMode` resume/deserialize), `media-viewer.js:~4448-4496` (`showTournamentPair`→`showCompareMedia`), `media-viewer.js:~4513` (`handleTournamentDraw` "Both Win"), `media-viewer.js:~2910-2940` (compare DOM teardown/reflow), `tournament-engine.js` (`deserialize`/SwissStrategy rebuild) [possible-dup-of: Speed up tournament launch & resume/continuation on large folders (24k+ files) — TODO (DONE 2026-06-24, PR #55); Speed up AI / similarity sorting on large folders (24k+ files) — TODO 🔴]
- [x] **Cannot enter tournament mode after adding new media + AI sort (tournament cache exists)** — ✅ **Resolved 2026-07-02** (Group CW-T, branch `fix/cw-t-tournament-hardening`; **real-24k manual smoke PASSED** — add-media + AI-sort → enter renders a pair, no "file missing"; merged via PR #59 `ae9588d`). Diagnosis corrected the hypothesis below: post-PR #55 the disk-resume path already reconciles, but the **live-engine fast-path** in `enterTournamentMode` skipped reconciliation → `getMediaIndex` −1. Fix: extracted `TournamentManager.reconcileWithFiles(currentFiles)` and call it in `_enterResumedTournamentUI` (covers every entry path, idempotent); hardened the −1 branch (bounded retry + `logError` divergence capture, now persisted to `media-viewer-perf.log`). **Subsumes** the [2026-06-25] `handleResumeReconciled` + `showTournamentPair`-missing-file durability items. See [archived plan](../archive/plans/2026-07-01-cw-t-tournament-correctness-hardening.md). _Original:_ high severity. After adding new media files to the folder and running Sort-by-Predicted, the user cannot enter tournament mode even though a saved `.tournament_state.json` exists. Likely root: AI sort reorders `mediaFiles` and the newly-added files are absent from the engine's saved file-set, so resume reconciliation + path→index lookup mismatch — `showTournamentPair` resolves `getMediaIndex(pair.left/right)` to -1 and falls into the "file missing" branch instead of rendering a pair. (После добавления новых медиа и ИИ сортировки не получается войти в турнирный режим(кеш турнира есть).); affected: `media-viewer.js:~4145` (`enterTournamentMode`), `tournament.js` (`handleResumeReconciled` reconciliation), `media-viewer.js:~4448-4470` (`showTournamentPair`→`getMediaIndex` -1 → "file missing" path), `media-viewer.js:~1090` (`getMediaIndex`) [possible-dup-of: Mid-tournament missing-file removal persists via debounce (`showTournamentPair`) — BACKLOG 🟤 [2026-06-25]; Reconciliation repair persists via debounce (`handleResumeReconciled`) — BACKLOG 🟤 [2026-06-25] — same code path, different trigger (sort reorder + new files vs persistence durability)]
- [ ] **AI sort on 24k: long opaque wait after "loading CLIP model" before extraction/sort begins** — medium severity. On a 24k folder, clicking Sort-by-Predicted shows a couple of "loading CLIP model" messages, then nothing for a long time; only after a long delay does a feature-extraction progress bar appear (finishing the remaining media), and only after it hits 100% does the sort run. Root: the ~40s streaming `loadFeatureCache()` runs silently with no progress UI before extraction/sort; the deterministic extraction progress bar appears only after it completes (the `kickoffBackgroundExtractionIfEnabled` toast auto-dismisses in 2s, then silence). (После открытия папки с 24000 файлами и нажатии на ИИ сортировку, то появляется пару сообщений о загрузке КЛИП модели и все, ничего больше не происходит, не загружается долгое время. Только через долгое время показівается feature extraction дорабатывает последние медиа и после 100% происходит сортировка); affected: `media-viewer.js:~7324` (`handleSortByPrediction`), `media-viewer.js:~7370`+`~6562-6683` (`loadFeatureCache` silent ~40s streaming load), `media-viewer.js:~8071` (`kickoffBackgroundExtractionIfEnabled` 2s toast then silence) [possible-dup-of: Speed up AI / similarity sorting on large folders (24k+ files) — TODO 🔴; Incremental feature-cache serving (~40s blocking load) — BACKLOG 🟤 [2026-05-26]]
- [ ] **AI sort re-runs feature extraction despite valid cached data on disk** — medium severity. On a 24k folder that already has a `.feature_cache.json`, clicking Sort-by-Predicted still shows a feature-extraction progress bar "finishing what wasn't done" — the user expects the cache to be used, not re-extracted. Likely root: `loadFeatureCache()` assigns the in-memory `featureCache` Map only at the END of the ~40s streaming load, so the uncached-file check (`!featureCache.has(path)`) that gates extraction runs against an empty/partial Map during the load window and triggers redundant extraction. (Да, все-таки ИИ сортировка крайне медленна на 24к+ файлов папке. Сразу после открітия папки, нажатия на кнопку сортировки, ничего не происходит продолжительное время, после появляется progress bar feature extraction(довыполняет то, что не было выполнено; feature extraction не выполнялся с начала, у папки есть кешированные данные), и сразу после 100% быстро показывается другое медиа с процентом.); affected: `media-viewer.js:~7370/~7392` (uncached-file check after `loadFeatureCache`), `media-viewer.js:~6644/~6675` (`featureCache` assigned only at end of streaming load), `media-viewer.js:~6563-6568` (single-flight) [possible-dup-of: Incremental feature-cache serving (~40s blocking load) — BACKLOG 🟤 [2026-05-26]; Speed up AI / similarity sorting on large folders (24k+ files) — TODO 🔴]

### [2026-06-13] JXL animation smoothness intake

**Origin**: Dogfooding the frame-0-first streaming decode (Group CW-5, PR #47). Frame 0 now appears fast, but the animation itself is not smooth — the user perceives it as playing "every n-th frame" (choppy/judders), and notes large source files (a 35 MB `.gif.jxl` plus other large gifs). Confirmed during the session that the playback loop is byte-for-byte identical to pre-CW-5 `main`, so this choppiness is **pre-existing**, not introduced by CW-5 — CW-5 only removed the long initial spinner that previously hid it.

- [ ] **Smooth animated-JXL playback via pipelined / look-ahead frame decode** — Root cause: `startJxlAnimation`'s loop decodes one PNG frame at a time _in the critical path_ (`await createImageBitmap(pngBlob)` → draw → `setTimeout(next, delay)`), so the real per-frame interval is `decode_time + delay` instead of `delay`. For high-FPS or high-resolution sources the decode latency exceeds the authored frame delay, dropping the achieved FPS well below the source rate → reads as stutter. The current design decodes on-demand deliberately (comment at [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) `startJxlAnimation`: holding all ~270 frames as raw bitmaps would be ~1 GB). **Fix**: decouple decode from display with a small bounded look-ahead — decode frame _i+1_ (and maybe _i+2_) while frame _i_ is on screen, holding a ring of 2-3 `ImageBitmap`s (~3 × raw-frame-size ≈ low tens of MB, not 1 GB). Optionally move `createImageBitmap` **into the worker** and transfer the `ImageBitmap` (transferable) so decode is fully off the main thread, and/or drive cadence with `requestAnimationFrame` + elapsed-time accounting for accurate timing. Smooth as long as average decode throughput ≥ playback rate; worst case (very large 4K/high-fps gifs) degrades to a _consistent_ slower rate rather than judder. Needs a design pass (brainstorm → spec): ring-buffer sizing, worker-vs-renderer decode split, interaction with the streaming `whenComplete` buffering gate, and the per-animation resident-PNG footprint vs the `jxlFrameCache` LRU cap (8) for large gifs. Effort: M. Affected: [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (`startJxlAnimation`, possibly `computeJxlFrameSchedule`), [jxl-decode-worker.js](../../jxl-decode-worker.js) (if decode moves worker-side). [related: [2026-06-07] frame-0-first streaming decode, shipped CW-5 / PR #47]

### [2026-06-07] Group A (JXL viewer) manual-testing intake

**Origin**: Manual smoke test of the shipped JXL viewer (branch `feature/jxl-viewer-support`). Static `.png/.jpg/.jpeg.jxl` render correctly with a clean console; animated `.gif.jxl` shows the first frame, but the user observed it "take very long time to load".

- [x] **Progressive / streaming decode for large animated JXL (frame-0-first)** — ✅ **Implemented 2026-06-12** (Group CW-5, branch `feature/jxl-progressive-decode`; see [DONE.md](DONE.md) + [archived plan](../archive/plans/2026-06-12-jxl-progressive-decode.md)). Worker now streams `{type:'meta'}` → `{type:'frame', id, index, pngBytes, duration}`×N → `{type:'done'}` (error possible mid-stream); `decodeJxl` resolves a mutable cache entry at frame-0 time (frames grow in place, `entry.whenComplete` settles at stream end); `startJxlAnimation` draws frame 0 immediately and starts the loop once buffered; mid-stream errors fall back to static frame 0. 297→310 unit tests. _Original entry:_ A 270-frame, 27 MB animated `.gif.jxl` decodes ALL frames in the worker before `decodeJxl` resolves (each frame → `encodeToPng`, ~77 MB total), so the spinner shows for several seconds before anything appears. Fix: stream frames — the worker posts frame 0 as soon as it's decoded so the renderer displays it immediately, then continues decoding the rest in the background and starts the loop once enough frames are buffered. This requires extending the worker protocol (e.g. a `{type:'frame', id, index, total, pngBytes, duration}` stream + a terminal `{type:'decoded'}`) and making `decodeJxl`/`startJxlAnimation` consume frames incrementally (resolve display on frame 0, accumulate for animation). Deliberately deferred from Group A to keep the initial PR focused on correctness. Effort: M. Affected: [jxl-decode-worker.js](../../jxl-decode-worker.js), [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (`decodeJxl`, `startJxlAnimation`).

- [ ] **Investigate "Both bad" convergence plateau (~15% left / ~2% right)** — Subjective report: repeatedly choosing "Both bad" on hard pairs makes subsequent rankings match the user's taste better, but the improvement plateaus once predictions saturate around 15% (left) / 2% (right). Likely expected behavior of online logistic regression under imbalanced negative feedback (boundary shifts down, scores saturate) rather than a bug. Worth checking whether the learning rate / regularization / class-imbalance handling in `OnlineLogisticRegression` over-weights dislike examples, and whether bulk-rated corrections should be down-weighted vs. real like/dislike moves. Effort: S (investigation). Affected: [ml-model.js](../../ml-model.js), [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (`updateMlModelWithFeatures`, `collectBulkRatedTrainingExamples`).

### [2026-05-30] Manual testing intake

**Origin**: Batch of UX/feature observations during AI-sorted compare-mode usage; bug-class items live in TODO.md Planned.

- [x] **Skip-pair handling: "Both good" + "Both bad" corrective-training buttons in AI-sorted compare** — ✅ **Implemented 2026-06-02** (Group 0 part 1, branch `feature/re-rate-mode-correction`; see [DONE.md](DONE.md) + [archived plan](../archive/plans/2026-05-31-rerate-compare-correction.md)). **Deviations from the original design below:** (1) soft-suppression / fall-through was **dropped** during brainstorming — bulk-rated files are treated as regular posts (no suppression, no badge); the re-pair-avoidance idea was re-raised during manual testing and is filed fresh under `### [2026-06-02]` below. (2) Shortcuts shipped as `bothGood: 'KeyD'` / `bothBad: 'KeyF'` with single+compare `next` remapped `KeyD→KeyS` (not the originally-proposed `KeyS`/`KeyD`). (3) Buttons live in a bottom-center `#compareActionBar` cluster (not grouped with `#cancelBtnCompare`). _Original design retained for reference:_ Two buttons grouped next to the existing cancel/undo button in the compare-mode special-button area ([index.html:~210](../../index.html#L210) `#cancelBtnCompare` region): **👍 Both good** and **👎 Both bad**. **Visibility**: only when `isSortedByPrediction === true && isCompareMode === true` (both Extremes and Sequential variants from the related compare-mode-variants item; hidden in single, tournament, and Similarity-sorted compare per user direction "В других режимах эта кнопка не будет логична"). **Mechanic**: each button calls `updateMlModelAfterRating(file, ±1)` for both files at full strength (same magnitude as a regular like/dislike — two training examples per click). Files **stay in the source folder** (no file move). **Original-intent context**: user designed this primarily for Extremes mode to correct cases where the AI sort paired items the model misclassified (predicted best-vs-worst but actually similar quality); Sequential support is opportunistic — same mechanic, different motivation. Bulk-rated files are **corrective training data**, not "judged-and-marked" — no file marker, no badge, files render identically to non-bulk-rated. **Persistence**: hydrated from per-folder `.bulk_rated.json` (`{ version: 1, good: [...], bad: [...] }`) on `loadFolder()`; persisted on every bulk-rating action via new IPC handlers `readBulkRatedFile`/`writeBulkRatedFile` (mirrors existing cache-file IPC pattern); a file is in at most one bucket — re-rating the same file the other direction moves it. Stale entries (file no longer in folder, e.g., renamed/moved externally) pruned silently on load. **Pair-selection: soft suppression with fall-through** — prefer pairs where neither file is in `bulkRatedSet`; fall back to bulk-rated-touching pairs when no fresh pairs remain so the user can re-rate (the model may still be wrong). No empty-state screen needed. **Cleanup**: file moved via like/dislike/special → purge from `bulkRatedSet` + re-save. **Re-rating**: clicking Both Good on an already-bulk-rated file just feeds another training update — supports iterative correction. **Undo (Ctrl+A)**: reverses both `updateMlModelAfterRating` calls, removes both files from `bulkRatedSet`, re-saves disk; history entries tagged `bothGood: true` / `bothBad: true` (analogous to existing `compareMode: true` tag). **Visual feedback**: 3s toast `"👍 Both files marked good (model updated)"` / `"👎 Both files marked bad (model updated)"`, mirroring existing rating UX. **Shortcuts**: `DEFAULT_SHORTCUTS.compare.bothGood: 'KeyS'`, `bothBad: 'KeyD'` (customizable via existing remap UI). **ML model rebuild**: `trainFromHistoricalRatingsAndWait()` gains a pass that re-applies `.bulk_rated.json` as ±1 training examples after walking the like/dislike folders, so corrections survive model reset. Pure-navigation skip remains a no-op for the model (explicit user-choice design — confirmed during brainstorming). (Если во время просмотра медиа в моде сравнения при ИИ сортировке пользователь не оценивает пару, а пропускает ее к следующей или предыдущей паре, то скорее всего пользователь не хочет оценивать пару, возможно потому что пара была не совсем корректно оценена, то есть либо оба хорошего качества и их сложно оценить, либо оба плохого. Можна добавить кнопку и дать выбор: "Оба хорошие" и "Оба плохие". Это кнопка может менять немного порядок постов при ИИ сортировке (влиять на оценку?). В других режимах эта кнопка не будет логична.) Effort: M. (В `C:\Users\alexm\Projects\media_compression` есть код, который компресит без потери качества некоторые расширения. Это можно добавить как добавляемый модуль. Но, скорее всего, его нужно будет переписать для кроссплатформенности.) Effort: L (after add-on system). Affected: new module + add-on plumbing; [main.js](../../main.js) (IPC for spawning compressor subprocesses), [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (UI invocation).
- [ ] **Two compare-mode variants: "Extremes" vs "Sequential" with sort-aware score chrome** — Compare mode gains a sub-toggle for pair-selection strategy. **Extremes** = pair `mediaFiles[i]` with `mediaFiles[N-1-i]` (best vs worst, walking both ends inward; terminates at `i >= floor(N/2)`). **Sequential** = pair `mediaFiles[2i]` with `mediaFiles[2i+1]` (top-down non-overlapping adjacent pairs; terminates at `2i+1 >= N`). Both fall back to "switch to single mode" when no more pairs remain (existing UX). **Default**: `'sequential'` for fresh users; last choice persisted to `localStorage.compareVariant` and restored in constructor. **Score chrome** (below each media in `.media-info` area, hidden when no sort active): AI sort → `Predicted: 87%`; Similarity CLIP → `Rank #N · CLIP dist: 0.12`; Similarity Hamming/MST/VPTree → `Rank #N` only (raw distance numbers are not user-friendly). **UI placement**: sub-toggle inside compare-controls area (reuse `.mode-btn` styling for visual parity with top mode selector); hidden in single + tournament modes. **State**: new `this.compareVariant` instance property (`'extremes'` | `'sequential'`); new `getComparePair()` helper replaces inline pair math in `showCompareMedia`; new `getScoreLabel(file)` helper branches on `isSortedByPrediction` / `isSortedBySimilarity` and `sortAlgorithm`. **Interactions**: tournament mode unaffected (Swiss-strategy engine pairing wins); mutual-exclusion item ([2026-05-07]) unaffected (variants operate on whatever order `mediaFiles` is currently in); bug #7 (TODO Planned, mode-switch desync) interacts — when fix path (b) wins (mediaFiles always reflects display order), `getComparePair` math simplifies because `mediaFiles[i]` and the score order align by construction. **Deferred**: per-mode keyboard shortcut to flip variant (e.g., `KeyV`); showing absolute score AND rank simultaneously; stride-N variant. (Можно переопределить текущий компар мод, как компар мод: хорошее и плохое (начало списка и конец) и добавить обычные компар мод (от хорошего до плохого). В каждом режиме использовать проценты и/или данные сортировочных алгоритмов, если они были использованы и предоставляются.) Effort: M-L. Affected: [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (new `compareVariant` state, `getComparePair`, `getScoreLabel`, `updateMediaInfo` wiring, `showCompareMedia` pair-selection rewrite, variant-toggle DOM init + click handler), [index.html](../../index.html) (variant toggle in compare-controls), [styles.css](../../styles.css) (toggle styling, score chrome), [tests/media-viewer-utils.test.js](../../tests/media-viewer-utils.test.js) (pair selection for both variants — odd N, even N, single-file edge), [tests/e2e/compare-mode.test.js](../../tests/e2e/compare-mode.test.js) (variant toggle + score chrome end-to-end).

### [2026-05-25] Tournament mode smoke-test intake

Two overhaul tasks identified during tournament-mode smoke test. Adding new
features keeps surfacing UI-layering and responsiveness issues that aren't worth
fixing one-at-a-time anymore — the system needs a coherent pass. Splitting into
two tasks because they have different shapes (architectural vs. visual).

- [ ] **UI architecture overhaul: mode-aware control system + z-index policy** — Multiple symptoms across features point to the same root cause: there's no coherent system for which UI elements are visible in which mode, or how they layer when they overlap. Concrete pain points: (1) tournament-mode adds a new "mode" but existing compare-mode controls (`.media-overlay-controls`, `.left/right-media-controls`) had to be hidden via reactive CSS rules per-element instead of declared once per mode; (2) the app `.header` (position: fixed, z-index: var(--z-modal)) covers the tournament header on hover because z-index values aren't centrally coordinated; (3) clicking the per-wrapper like/dislike buttons in tournament mode routed to like-folder moves because the buttons exist on the wrapper and stopPropagation, so the tournament pick handler never sees the click (fixed reactively in commit `9f74c64`/`<next-commit>`); (4) media info, file count, and tournament progress all live in different containers with no unified placement system. Proposed: a mode-aware control registry (each mode declares its visible controls + their roles); a z-index scale formalized as CSS custom properties (`--z-overlay-low`, `--z-overlay-high`, `--z-modal`, `--z-tooltip`); convert per-wrapper buttons to a single shared bottom-bar that re-renders its contents based on mode rather than overlaying multiple button sets. Effort: L. Affected: [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (control rendering, mode switching), [styles.css](../../styles.css) (z-index variables, control layout), [index.html](../../index.html) (control containers). Likely a 2-day refactor; do before adding the 4th mode (RoundRobin or Bracket strategy).
- [ ] **Responsive design pass: handle different window sizes, media counts, and tier counts** — The app was built assuming a typical desktop window with a moderate number of files. Stress-testing tournament with 24k files surfaced cases where the UI doesn't scale: progress text overflows when tier counts get long (e.g., `Tiers: 0·0·1·24832` runs off-screen on narrow windows); the file count in the folder-info pill doesn't truncate when N is large; media overlay controls don't reflow when the window narrows; tournament config modal estimates assume ~5sec/game which is wrong at extreme N. Proposed pass: (a) define breakpoints (narrow / typical / wide) and reflow rules for each; (b) truncate numeric displays with a thousands separator + tooltip showing exact count; (c) tier breakdown layout switches to vertical list when string length exceeds container width; (d) modal sizes use `max-width: min(520px, 90vw)` instead of fixed widths; (e) test fixtures include extreme-N case (e.g., 10k file E2E). Effort: M-L. Affected: [styles.css](../../styles.css) (media queries + flex/grid for control bars), [media-viewer.js](../../media-viewer.js) (number formatting, tier breakdown rendering in TournamentManager.getTierBreakdownText). Do AFTER the UI architecture overhaul — easier to apply consistent responsive rules once the layering is rationalized.

### [2026-05-07] Manual repro session intake (Group A)

**Origin**: User executed the 8-step manual repro for `fix/clip-extraction-silent-failure` (PR #34) and confirmed the CLIP extraction fix works end-to-end. Two unrelated UI bugs surfaced during the session.

- [x] ~~**Compare-mode → folder-switch leaves stale media wrappers visible**~~ — ✅ Resolved 2026-06-09 by Group B (`feature/mode-switch-display-bugs`). `switchToSingleModeUI()` now tears down the `.left-media-wrapper`/`.right-media-wrapper` nodes (`fullscreen.cleanup` + `.remove()` + null) on every exit-to-single path; redundant inline teardowns in `moveComparePair` + `showCompareMedia` removed. E2E asserts no stale wrappers after folder switch. See [DONE.md](DONE.md#2026-06-09--group-b-mode-switch-display-bugs) and [archived plan](../archive/plans/2026-06-08-mode-switch-display-bugs.md). Original report below. — Repro: enter Compare Mode (2 media files side-by-side), then select a different folder via the change-folder button. Observed: new folder loads in single-file mode (correct — `loadFolder()` calls `switchToSingleModeUI()` before `hideDropZone()` per the existing CLAUDE.md gotcha), BUT the two previous compare-mode media wrappers remain visible, shifted to the left of the viewport and shrunk to small dimensions while the new single-mode media renders to their right. See screenshot in PR #34 thread (vertical red-shirt image right-side, faint shifted/shrunk wrappers left). Suggests `switchToSingleModeUI()` reverts the mode flag and overlay controls but does not remove/hide the leftover `.compare-wrapper` / `.media-wrapper-left` / `.media-wrapper-right` DOM nodes. Likely fix: `switchToSingleModeUI()` should `.remove()` the compare wrappers from `mediaContainer` (or hide them) before the new media renders. Affected: `media-viewer.js` (`switchToSingleModeUI` ~near `loadFolder`/`toggleViewMode` call sites), `styles.css` (compare-wrapper layout). E2E coverage: extend existing `compare-mode.test.js` "resets to single mode when switching folders" to also assert `.compare-wrapper` is not in the DOM after the switch.
- [ ] **Hash sort + AI sort are not mutually exclusive — separate undo for each is confusing** — Repro: load a folder, click Sort-by-Similarity (any algorithm — VPTree/MST/CLIP), then click Sort-by-Prediction (AI). Observed: both sorts apply in sequence and the user can undo each independently (two separate "Restore Order" affordances). Today they're independent buttons with independent state (`isSortedBySimilarity` + `isSortedByPrediction`) so the undo paths don't interlock. User-suggested fix: unify both into a single Sort menu/dropdown where the algorithm options become `[Similarity (Hamming MST/VPTree/CLIP), AI (Prediction)]` — selecting one displaces the other, and a single "Restore Original Order" button affects whichever sort is active. Alternative: keep the two buttons but make the "active sort" state mutually exclusive — clicking AI-sort while similarity-sorted first restores similarity then applies AI, with a single undo. Affected: `media-viewer.js` (`handleSortBySimilarity` ~L4140+, `handleSortByPrediction` ~L6271+, `originalMediaFiles`/`mediaFiles` swap logic, the two `.btn-label` toggles for "Sort by …"/"Restore Order"), `index.html` (sort-related buttons + algorithm dropdown), possibly `styles.css`. Open design question: does the AI sort go into the existing algorithm `<select>` (`vptree | mst | simple | clip | prediction`), or does the menu get a separate "sort source" axis (similarity vs prediction)? **[Strengthened 2026-05-30]**: user re-reported this on a separate occasion ("Я уже раньше писал, но ИИ сортировка и другие должны быть взаимоисключающие друг друга — чтобы нельзя было использовать ИИ сортировку и другую друг за другом без отмены предыдущей или, хотя бы, без указания, что текущая сортировка будет отменена"). Confirms minimum requirement: applying a second sort without first undoing the active one should at minimum **prompt** the user that the current sort will be replaced. Promote priority within the entry.

### [2026-05-05] Manual testing intake

**Origin**: User feature ideas and UX observations during manual session

- [ ] **Add 3-media batch rating mode** — New mode where the user rates 3 media simultaneously: explicitly chooses one for like, one for dislike, leaves the third unrated (stays in folder, not moved). Differs from current compare mode (with 2 media) where rating one auto-rates the other; here the user must assign two ratings explicitly. Could speed up sorting throughput. Optional follow-on: a sort variant that treats the "stays" choice as a third label, but unseen files cannot count as "unrated stays" (Новый режим: оценка 3х медиа сразу. Пользователь выбирает: кому ставится лайк, кому дизлайк, а кто остается не оценен и не перемещается. По сути, очень похоже на режиме с 2мя оценками, только теперь пользователь должен определять 2 оценки, а не одну (потому что в режиме с 2мя можно определить одну оценку, а вторая поставится автоматически). Я предполагаю, что такой режим может позволит, как минимум, быстрее сортировать. Можно также добавить сортировку для такого режима, которая будет учитывать те медиа, которые остаются в папке (третья оценка; но при этом те, которые еще не были показаны, не могут считаться таковыми)); affected: `media-viewer.js` (new mode alongside `isCompareMode`/single, ~L345/L1094 mode state, `showCompareMedia` ~L2451+, rating handlers), `index.html` (3-pane layout), `styles.css` (3-pane CSS)
- [ ] **Allow ML model retrain (not just restore order)** — Today the user can only restore the original pre-prediction order; no UI affordance to force a model retrain from accumulated ratings. Open design question: does the model retrain incrementally during ratings (current behavior of `updateMlModelAfterRating`), or also on folder open after enough new ratings accumulate across sessions? After each rating session the like/dislike folders gain content from prior decisions and the main folder shrinks — that drift may warrant explicit "retrain from scratch" UX (Возможность переобучить модель (а не только восстановить старый порядок). Модель дообучается во время оценок или во время открытия папки? Т.е. происходит переобучение, если открывать одну и ту же папку, но после какого-то количества оценок (лайк и дизлайк фолдеры дополняются медиа из прошлых оценок той папки, которая выбирается)? То есть после каждой сессии оценок меняется наполнение основной папки, наполнение папок лайков и дизлайков); affected: `media-viewer.js:~L5730` (`resetMlModel`), `~L6271` (`handleSortByPrediction`), `trainFromHistoricalRatingsAndWait`, Settings panel (F1)
- [ ] **Overlay buttons have 3-stage "jumping" hover/leave transition** — On hover-in then hover-out, the `.media-overlay-controls` buttons cycle through 3 visually distinct states: transparent-bg buttons appear → glass effect (backdrop-filter) kicks in → glass holds while hovered → on mouse-leave glass disappears first, transparent buttons remain briefly, then buttons fade out. The staged transition reads as broken/attention-grabbing. Likely cause: `transition-delay: 500ms` on `.media-overlay-controls` opacity combines with separate `backdrop-filter` transition on `.overlay-btn` and per-button `:hover` background swaps — three transitions on different timelines. Fix: unify into one coordinated transition or remove the staggered delay on hide (Кнопки управления медиа имеют 3 состаяния, которые "прыгают": При навидении показываются кнопки с прозрачным фоном, потом быстро появляется эффект стекла и держится, пока медиа в фокусе (мышка наведена), после переноса мышки с медиа эффект стекла пропадает, появляется прозрачный фон и уже после кнопки пропадают. Проблема: Это выглядит как-будто так не должно быть, ощущения "прыгающих кнопок" очень привлекающих к себе внимание); affected: [styles.css:1623-1716](styles.css#L1623-L1716) (`.media-overlay-controls` `transition-delay: 500ms`, `.overlay-btn` `transition: all`, per-btn `:hover` background swaps)
- [ ] **Add rotation buttons for media** — Some media files (static images, animated images, videos) display rotated incorrectly. Add manual rotation controls (e.g., 90° CW/CCW buttons) so the user can correct orientation per-file for comfortable viewing/rating. Persistence question: ephemeral (per-session) or saved per-file? (Некоторые медиа (статические или анимированные, в том числе видео) иногда перевернуты. Можно добавить кнопки поворота медиа для комфорта); affected: `media-viewer.js` (`showSingleMedia`/`showCompareMedia`, new rotation state per file or per-mediaFiles entry), `index.html` (rotate buttons — possibly in overlay controls), `styles.css` (CSS `transform: rotate()` on media element + interaction with existing zoom transforms)
- [ ] **Move media to arbitrary folder via special button** — Add a button to move the current media to any folder the user picks (beyond the fixed like/dislike/special destinations). Folder picker via existing IPC dialog (or a new "recent destinations" picker for speed). History entry needed for undo (Дать возможность переместить медиа в любую папку по спец кнопке); affected: `media-viewer.js` (new handler + folder-picker IPC call + `moveHistory` entry), `main.js` (likely reusable existing file-move IPC; possibly new "browse for folder" IPC if not exposed), `index.html` (button placement — overlay controls or main controls bar), `preload.js`

### [2026-05-03] Manual smoke test intake (clip-sort-followups)

**Origin**: User attempted Scenario 1 of the spec's manual test plan on a 63-file image folder (`Act2_Warm`) and discovered CLIP extraction is not firing on folder load. The branch's actual scope (cache-hit insertion + toggle-off cleanup) is verified by 167/167 unit tests + 39/39 E2E + final code review, but downstream manual scenarios (1-3, 5-6) are blocked by this pre-existing issue.

- [x] ~~🔴 **CLIP background extraction silently does not fire on folder load**~~ — ✅ Resolved 2026-05-07 by Group A (`fix/clip-extraction-silent-failure`). Root cause: `startBackgroundFeatureExtraction()` had no call site in `loadFolder()`. Fix: new `kickoffBackgroundExtractionIfEnabled()` method called after `updateFolderInfo()`. See [DONE.md](DONE.md#2026-05-07-group-a-clip-extraction-silent-failure) and [archived plan](../archive/plans/2026-05-06-clip-extraction-silent-failure.md).
- [x] **Add UX-visible "extraction starting" notification** — ✅ Resolved 2026-06-10 (Group C, PR #45 `ad4e488`). Even if extraction is firing, the absence of any visible "Loading CLIP model..." or "Extracting features: 0/63" feedback for the first ~1-30 seconds creates a "did anything happen?" moment that prompted the bug above to be reported. Adding a "Starting feature extraction..." toast immediately on folder load (before any per-file progress) would surface failure modes faster and improve perceived responsiveness. Cosmetic but high UX value (now unblocked since 2026-05-07 Group A merged).
- [x] **Toggle-on kickoff for CLIP** (deferred from Group A spec) — ✅ Resolved 2026-06-10 (Group C, PR #45 `ad4e488`). When the user toggles CLIP **on** in Settings while a folder is already loaded, today's behavior is implicit: nothing happens until they switch folders or click a CLIP-dependent sort. Add a kickoff so toggle-on triggers the same `kickoffBackgroundExtractionIfEnabled()` path as folder load. Affected: `media-viewer.js` (`#clipFeaturesToggle` change handler — currently `async` with revert-before-await cleanup on toggle-off; add inverse op).

## 🟡 Operational & Observation Items

### [2026-08-27] Weekly-Reviews adopt queue — burn-down & cadence (1 item)

**Origin**: Recorded by the 2026-08-27 Weekly Reviews run (3rd) as a standing planning signal rather
than a defect. Periodic-maintenance in nature → 🟡, not 🟤.

- [x] **Burn down the Weekly-Reviews adopt-trial queue, and settle the cadence** — `adopt` verdicts have accumulated across all three runs with **zero burn-down**: `pr-review-toolkit` (2026-06-26), `typescript-lsp` + autonomous/visual verification (2026-07-05), and `security-guidance` + `dead-rules-audit` + path-scoped-rules migration (2026-08-27) — **5 unchecked**. This is not a process failure: decision **D4 keeps `adopt` deliberately hands-off** (file a trial entry, install nothing), so the queue is behaving exactly as specified. The gap is that **nothing consumes it**, and a review process whose output is never tried produces verdicts, not value. Decide between: (a) gate new adopts on trialling the outstanding ones, (b) schedule a periodic trial batch (a natural Cleanup-Week slot — several are single-command installs), or (c) accept the queue as a low-priority reading list and stop counting it as work. Second, related: the "**weekly**" cadence has actually run 2026-06-26 → 2026-07-05 → 2026-08-27, the last gap being **~7.5 weeks** — decide whether this is a weekly batch that keeps slipping or a **~monthly** one that should be planned as such, since the scan window ("since the last run", not "this week") already assumes the latter. Cross-ref: REVIEW-QUEUE.md §§1–4.
  ✅ **Resolved 2026-08-30 (Group G4).** Both halves decided and recorded in [REVIEW-QUEUE.md](REVIEW-QUEUE.md) § 5 Adopt trials + 📌 Process Rules above. **(a/b/c) → option b+**: periodic trial batches, and an `adopt` must name its trial vehicle at filing time or be filed `defer`; queue cap of 3 outstanding. **Cadence → weekly, as written** (user decision) — the 2026-06-26 → 07-05 → 08-27 slippage is kept as a watch, to be re-opened with evidence if a fourth long gap occurs, not silently re-timed. Two counting errors in this entry corrected while there: it says "5 unchecked" but names **six** (it drops the path-scoped-rules migration), and `security-guidance` was **already installed and enabled on 2026-08-24**, three days before the run that adopted it — so the true state was 6 filed / 1 already live / 5 outstanding — 4 once this same commit closes `pr-review-toolkit`, and the one item that got consumed was consumed outside the process. That is the evidence the b+ decision rests on.

### [2026-07-02] Vitest v4 full-suite worker flake (dev-only, intermittent)

**Origin**: Surfaced repeatedly during the Group CW-T subagent run — `npx vitest run` (full 15-file parallel run) intermittently reports `15 failed / no tests` ("Vitest failed to find the runner" / "No test suite found", vitest v4.0.18) on this Windows machine, while single-file runs (`npx vitest run <substring>`) and `--no-file-parallelism` are **reliable**, and the pre-commit hook's `npx vitest run` has passed on every commit. Low urgency (hook + sequential runs work), but it costs retries on every local verification. Effort: S. Affected: [vitest.config.js](../../vitest.config.js), possibly `package.json` test scripts.
